Thus to prevent beneficial swap, we need to ensure that any student X that A could swap with does not get increased satisfaction by moving to A's group. That seems hard because many students rate A relatively high (e.g., F rates A4, D1, G1, H3, I3, B2, C3, E1). Only D,G,E rate A low (1).
